inspection: years are in row 4, columns H-L (8-12)\nyear_row = 4\nyear_cols = list(range(8, 13))  # H..L\n\nyears = [ws_data.cell(year_row, c).value for c in year_cols]\nif any(y is None for y in years):\n    raise RuntimeError(f'Unexpected missing year headers at Data row {year_row}, cols {year_cols}: {years}')\n\nseries_col = 4  # column D\nstart_row, end_row = 21, 38\n\nlookup = {}\nfor r in range(start_row, end_row + 1):\n    series = ws_data.cell(r, series_col).value\n    if not series:\n        continue\n    for c, year in zip(year_cols, years):\n        val = ws_data.cell(r, c).value\n        lookup[(str(series), int(year))] = val\n\n# --- Helper: percentile.inc ---\ndef percentile_inc(values, k):\n    xs = sorted(values)\n    n = len(xs)\n    if n == 0:\n        return None\n    if k <= 0:\n        return xs[0]\n    if k >= 1:\n        return xs[-1]\n    rank = 1 + (n - 1) * k\n    lo = int(rank)  # floor\n    hi = lo + 1\n    frac = rank - lo\n    # 1-indexed\n    x_lo = xs[lo - 1]\n    x_hi = xs[hi - 1] if hi <= n else xs[-1]\n    return x_lo + frac * (x_hi - x_lo)\n\n# --- Step 1: write INDEX/MATCH formulas in three blocks ---\n# Data ranges fixed: Data!$H$21:$L$38; row match on Data!$D$21:$D$38; col match on Data!$H$4:$L$4\n\ndef set_lookup_formulas(row_start, row_end):\n    for r in range(row_start, row_end + 1):\n        for col in range(8, 13):  # H..L\n            col_letter = get_column_letter(col)\n            addr = f'{col_letter}{r}'\n            # Example: =INDEX(Data!$H$21:$L$38, MATCH($D12, Data!$D$21:$D$38, 0), MATCH(H$10, Data!$H$4:$L$4, 0))\n            formula = (\n                f\"=INDEX(Data!$H${start_row}:$L${end_row}, \"\n                f\"MATCH($D{r}, Data!$D${start_row}:$D${end_row}, 0), \"\n                f\"MATCH({col_letter}$10, Data!$H${year_row}:$L${year_row}, 0))\"\n            )\n            ws_task[addr].value = formula\n\nset_lookup_formulas(12, 17)\nset_lookup_formulas(19, 24)\nset_lookup_formulas(26, 31)\n\n# --- Step 2: net reliability gap formulas H35:L40 ---\nfor r in range(35, 41):\n    success_r = r - 23\n    fail_r = r - 16\n    cap_r = r - 9\n    for col in range(8, 13):\n        cl = get_column_letter(col)\n        ws_task[f'{cl}{r}'].value = f\"=({cl}{success_r}-{cl}{fail_r})/{cl}{cap_r}*100\"\n\n# --- Step 2b: summary statistics H42:L47 ---\nstat_rows = {\n    42: 'MIN',\n    43: 'MAX',\n    44: 'MEDIAN',\n    45: 'AVERAGE',\n    46: 'PERCENTILE',\n    47: 'PERCENTILE',\n}\nfor col in range(8, 13):\n    cl = get_column_letter(col)\n    rng = f'=AVERAGE({rng})'\n    ws_task[f'{cl}46'].value = f'=PERCENTILE({rng},0.25)'\n    ws_task[f'{cl}47'].value = f'=PERCENTILE({rng},0.75)'\n\n# --- Step 3: weighted mean H50:L50 ---\nfor col in range(8, 13):\n    cl = get_column_letter(col)\n    ws_task[f'{cl}50'].value = f'=SUMPRODUCT({cl}35:{cl}40,{cl}26:{cl}31)/SUM({cl}26:{cl}31)'\n\n# --- Step 4: compute numeric values for all formula cells we wrote ---\n# Compute the block lookups first\ncomputed = {}  # cell_ref -> numeric\n\n# Years from Task row 10 should match Data header\ntask_years = [ws_task.cell(10, c).value for c in range(8, 13)]\n\n# sanity\nif [int(y) for y in task_years] != [int(y) for y in years]:\n    # still proceed, but map by actual task_years; match should still work\n    pass\n\nfor r in list(range(12, 18)) + list(range(19, 25)) + list(range(26, 32)):\n    series = ws_task[f'D{r}'].value\n    if series is None:\n        continue\n    series = str(series)\n    for c in range(8, 13):\n        year = int(ws_task.cell(10, c).value)\n        key = (series, year)\n        if key not in lookup:\n            raise KeyError(f'Missing Data value for series/year: {key}')\n        val = lookup[key]\n        computed[f'{get_column_letter(c)}{r}'] = float(val) if val is not None else None\n\n# Net reliability gap rows 35-40\nfor r in range(35, 41):\n    success_r = r - 23\n    fail_r = r - 16\n    cap_r = r - 9\n    for c in range(8, 13):\n        cl = get_column_letter(c)\n        success = computed[f'{cl}{success_r}']\n        fail = computed[f'{cl}{fail_r}']\n        cap = computed[f'{cl}{cap_r}']\n        if cap in (0, 0.0, None) or success is None or fail is None:\n            val = None\n        else:\n            val = (success - fail) / cap * 100.0\n        computed[f'{cl}{r}'] = val\n\n# Summary stats rows 42-47\nimport statistics\nfor c in range(8, 13):\n    cl = get_column_letter(c)\n    vals = [computed[f'{cl}{r}'] for r in range(35, 41)]\n    if any(v is None for v in vals):\n
